## Runner's own moments (player view, Fold 755x1000, persona skilled = core gamer) — 21-year life, 3245 buổi
- y5-y6 · #kmrew / .kmov · rank 0 → 1 → 0 (m-rank-626-51.png, m-rank-746-14.png): promoted at year 5 and demoted at year 6 without a visible reason — CONFUSED/ODD; after that rank 0 for 15 years
- y5-y21 · FRUSTRATED · Fold gift pickers: «Biếu quà làm lành» and «Cài đồ vào rương» — the item grid's confirm «kipgo» stays off-screen after 8 real scrolls (two dialogs); board theu: KG82.phase stays «play» after the round ended (result button up, box hidden); 8 tab crashes, 1 SAVE REWIND; the SAVE WALL (QuotaExceededError, 5.4 M chars) from buổi ~2450
- y21 end · DELIGHTED/ODD · «Xuân tàn lãnh cung — Mở Sổ luân hồi» fades in over a rich main screen at age 40: Phế phi Kiều Hải Lan, lãnh cung, «Cấm túc 1 tháng», Bạc 163, a Túi đồ of 1484 items (Trang sức 13, Vải vóc 241 …) — a whole life of collected things and nothing to spend them on; «Tiếp theo» half-visible under the Tư trang tab · shots-R2-skilled-3/end.png
